Mirror step details:
```
def mirror_step(a, eta, tau, topK, c, cmax):
    n=len(a)
    q=make_weights(c,cmax,tau,topK)
    D=grad_D(a,q,n)
    dmax=max(D.max(), cmax, 1e-300)
    ratio = D/dmax
    # D nonnegative. To avoid all variables depleted, subtract weighted average? Multiplicative with normalization: a*exp(-eta*D). This pushes all down, but renormalizes low D up. Good.
    a_new = a * np.exp(-eta * ratio)
    floor = 1e-12 / n? maybe 1e-300
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-300)
    a_new /= a_new.sum()
    return a_new
```

```
def shape_update(a, alpha=0.5, eps_frac=1e-2, window=None):
    n=len(a); N=pow2(2n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2n-1]
    # create target delta q: want reduce c where above reference.
    # reference: smoothed c via real FFT Gaussian? simpler box window length w = max(3, int(0.05*(2n-1)))
    c_smooth = circular? linear conv? Use np.convolve(c, box, 'same').
    delta = c - c_smooth
    # emphasize peaks: delta = np.maximum(delta, 0) - np.mean(np.maximum(delta,0))? Sum zero? If subtract mean of positive, c_smooth offset? We can just set delta = delta - delta.mean(), then maybe keep positive peaks negative lows.
    # scale delta to have max 1 maybe.
    maxd = max(abs(delta.max()),eps)
    delta = delta / maxd
    D=np.fft.rfft(delta,N)
    denom=np.abs(A)**2 + eps_frac*np.max(np.abs(A)**2)
    H=np.conj(A)*D/(2.0*denom)
    h=np.fft.irfft(H,N, n)  # first n coefficients
    # h may have huge; scale step so max |h| <= alpha * a.mean()/ (max(abs(a))? maybe)
    hscale = alpha * (a.mean()) / max(np.max(np.abs(h)), eps)
    a_new = a + hscale*h
    a_new = np.clip(a_new,0,1e10)
    a_new /= a_new.sum()
    return a_new
```
This may reduce variance of c. But does it reduce max? It tries to reduce peaks relative smoothed. Need line search: evaluate new score; if worse, try smaller alpha or revert. Excess = max(0,c - T). If too aggressive, many lags. We want reduce top lags. Let `T = cmax - 0.1*cmax =0.9cmax` etc. Sum excess positive. To keep sum zero, subtract excess sum from all lags: delta=excess - excess.sum()/(2n-1). Then peaks above T get positive delta; all lags get negative uniform offset. This reduces peaks more than lows. Good. For step, h from delta.

`def conv_full(a):` assumes normalized length n; N=pow2(2n-1); A=rfft(a,N); c=irfft(A*A,N)[:2n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0) return c.

`def make_weights(c, cmax, tau, topK):`
- cmax positive. If topK is not None and topK>0:
   - ind=np.argpartition(c, -topK)[-topK:]
   - q=zeros; q[ind]=1/topK; return.
   But to include near max threshold maybe use topK over values > cmax - tol. Simpler topK.
- else:
   - if tau <=0: uniform over argmax? Actually tau zero -> top1.
   - x = -(c - cmax)/ (tau*cmax) (>=0). To avoid overflow, x=np.minimum(x, 700). q=np.exp(x); q/=q.sum(); return.
If tau is relative to cmax. For cmax zero return uniform.

`def grad_D(a,q,n):`
- rev_a=a[::-1]
- L=len(q)+n-1 = (2n-1)+n-1=3n-2
- N=pow2(L)
- A=rfft(rev_a,N); Q=rfft(q,N)
- D_full=irfft(A*Q,N)[:L]
- D=2.0*D_full[n-1:2n-1]  # length n
- return D
Check slice n-1 to 2n-1 inclusive -> length n. In Python `D_full[n-1:2*n-1]`.

Could optimize by precomputing rfft(rev_a) but a changes. Fine.

`def mirror_update(a, eta, tau, topK, momentum_state, mom):`

- q=make_weights
- D=grad_D
- If D.max()>0:
   Dscale=max(D.max(), cmax, 1e-300)

   if momentum_state is None: state=r.copy(); else state=mom*state + (1-mom)*r
   # Optional: subtract weighted mean? Use natural gradient? Let's try both. We can add flag `center`: if center, state = state - np.mean(state*a)? Actually for multiplicitive, state nonnegative.    clip state to [0, 100]

- return a_new
```

`def fourier_h(theta, n, M, base_zero=True):`

Function:
```
def fourier_obj(theta, n, M, tau, V, exact_beta=None):
    h = V @ theta  # V shape n x 2M
    a = softmax(h)
    c=conv_full(a)
    # LSE score with tau: L = logsumexp(c/tau)*tau? c scale 1/n. Use tau_abs.
    # gradient wrt h: D from weights q with exact? Need compute grad_h = a*D - a*(a·D) times factor.
    q=make_weights(c, cmax, tau, 0) # topK=0 means softmax weights
    D=grad_D(a,q,n)
    # dF/dh = 2n*(a*D - a*(a·D))? But objective LSE F=2nL with L=logsumexp(c/tau)*tau. dL/da=D. dF/dh=2n( a D - a (aD) ). We can scale out 2n.
    weighted=a.dot(D)
    grad_h=a*D - a*weighted
    # If using exact score, weights top1; LSE okay.
    L=cmax + tau*logsumexp((c-cmax)/tau)
   return 2*n*L, grad_h
```
Then theta gradient: `grad_theta = V.T @ grad_h` because h=Vθ. For softmax normalized, Vθ shift-invariant; okay.

`def smooth_noise(n, rng, corr):`
- z=rng.standard_normal(n)
- convolve with kernel length max(3, int(corr*n)) / len. Or FFT lowpass: z_hat=rfft(z); mask=exp(-idx^2/w^2); z=irfft(z_hat*mask)/std.

For n large, direct conv O(n w) maybe okay if w=100. Or FFT.

`def random_perturbation(a, rng, sigma)`:
```
loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
noise=smooth_noise(len(a), rng, order=1)
noise = noise - noise.mean()
loga += sigma*noise
a=np.exp(loga - loga.max()); normalize
```

`def shape_direction(a, c, target_frac, eps_frac, N=...):`
- n; L=2n-1; N=pow2(L)
- A=rfft(a,N)
- T=c.max()*target_frac (e.g., 0.85).
- excess=np.maximum(c-T,0.0)
- if excess.sum()>0:
    delta=excess.copy()
    delta -= np.mean(delta) # sum zero
    # Maybe lowpass delta to reduce high h:
    # Use rfft delta, multiply by window decay in frequency: freq=...; mask=exp(-(freq/f0)^2); D*=mask. f0 maybe N/20.
    D=rfft(delta,N)
    # mask for first N/2 freqs
    freq=np.arange(N//2+1); fmax=N/2? Actually DHermitian; mask[0]=1; mask=exp(-(freq/(N*0.1))**2). D*=mask[0:len(D)].
    denom=np.abs(A)**2 + eps_frac*np.max(np.abs(A)**2)
    H=np.conj(A)*D/(2*denom)
    h=irfft(H, N)[:n]
  else zero.
- return h

Function `fourier_eval(theta,V,tau_rel,n)`:
- h=V.dot(theta); a=normalize(softmax)
- c=conv_full; cmax
- L=cmax + tau_abs*logsumexp((c-cmax)/tau_abs) where tau_abs=max(cmax*tau_rel,1e-300)
- score=2nL
- q=make_weights(c,cmax,tau_rel,None)
- D=grad_D
- weighted=a.dot(D)
- grad_h=a*D - a*weighted
- grad_theta=V.T.dot(grad_h)
- return score, grad_theta, a

`def mirror_optimize(a0, time_budget, deadline, eta_start, eta_end, tau_start, tau_end, topK_start, topK_end, mom, stall_iter, do_shape=False, check_sym=True):`
- a=normalize(a0). best=a copy; best_score=internal.
- state=None
- stall=0
- t0=monotonic; limit=t0+time_budget
- steps=0
- while monotonic < min(deadline, limit):
   - c=conv_full(a); cmax=c.max(); cur=2n*cmax
   - update best if cur<best (also sym: bs=sym(a); sc=s; if better)
   - f=elapsed/budget
   - tau_rel = tau_start*(tau_end/tau_start)**f
   - topK = None if f<0.7 else int(topK_end)
   - eta = eta_start*(eta_end/eta_start)**f
   - q=make_weights; D=grad_D
   - Dscale=max(D.max(), cmax, 1e-300)
   - r=D/Dscale
   - if state None state=r; else state=mom*state+(1-mom)*r
   - a = a * exp(-eta*state); normalize with floor
   - steps++
   - every 200: if cur > best_score*1.01? Actually cur current before update, best updated. To detect stall: if best_score not improved since last_check and steps-last_best > stall_iter, perturb. We need track last_best_steps. At start last_best=0. When improve, last_best=steps.
   - if steps-last_best > stall_iter:
        # perturb best slightly or current? Use best with noise.
        a = random_perturbation(best, rng, sigma=0.1+0.1*rng.random())
        state=None
        # try shape update if budget?
        if do_shape and steps%...:
             a2=shape_accept(a,c,...)
             if shape_score<score: a=a2
        # restart against best, keep last_best? set last_best=steps? To allow another stall.
   - maybe every 5000 blend with best: a=(1-0.1)a+0.1best.
- return best_score, best_a
```

`smooth_noise` top-level:
```
def smooth_noise(n, rng, scale=1.0):
   z=rng.standard_normal(n)
   # FFT lowpass
   N=next_pow2(n)
   Z=np.fft.rfft(z,N)
   freq=np.arange(N//2+1)
   sigma = max(1.0, n/16.0)
   mask=np.exp(-0.5*(freq/sigma)**2)
   mask[0]=1
   Z*=mask[0:len(Z)]
   z=np.fft.irfft(Z,N)[:n]
   z=(z-z.mean())
   std=z.std()
   if std>0: z*=scale/std
   else...
   return z
```

Upsampling:
```
def upsample(a,new_n):
   n=len(a)
   if n==new_n: return a.copy()
   loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
   # For periodic? sequence not periodic; use linear.
   lognew=np.interp(x_new,x_old,loga)
   new=exp(lognew-lognew.max()); normalize with floor.
```
If a has sharp peaks, interpolation may smooth; okay.

Potential problem: The objective with linear convolution not autocorrelation: Finger `grad_D` formula correct? For c_k = ∑_i a_i a_{k-i}. Derivative wrt a_j: For each k, if 0≤k-j<n and 0≤j<n, derivative includes a_{k-j}; also if j appears as second factor i? c_k sum over i; second factor index =k-i. Setting k-i=j => i=k-j. If i=j? Same term. For i != k-j, there are two terms: i=j gives a_j a_{k-j}; i=k-j gives a_{k-j} a_j. Both derivative a_{k-j}. If i=j and i=k-j same (2j=k), term a_j^2 derivative 2a_j. But for c_k of sequence with itself, the terms are ordered pairs; derivative includes both order. Good.

Detailed function specifications:

`def shift_deadline(deadline, margin):` returns max(0,deadline-margin) no need.

`def get_fft_len(m):`
```
r=1
while r<m: r <<=1
return r
```

`def softmax_from_h(h):`
```
h=np.asarray(h,dtype=np.float64)
h=h-h.max()
a=np.exp(h)
s=a.sum()
if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return np.ones(len(h))/len(h)
a/=s
return a
```

`def normalize_seq(a):`
```
a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
if a.size==0: return None
a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
s=float(a.sum())
if s<=1e-300: return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
a=a/s
floor=1e-15/max(1,a.size)
a=np.maximum(a,floor)
a/=a.sum()
return a
```
Floor after normalization: if floor>0, a values all at least floor. For n=16384 floor=6e-17. Good.

`def conv_linear(a):`
```
n=a.size
L=2*n-1
N=get_fft_len(L)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)
return c[:L]
```
Maybe round negative small to 0: `c=np.maximum(c,0.0)`.

```

`def build_fourier_matrix(n, M):`
```
idx=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
V=np.empty((n,2*M), dtype=np.float64)
for m in range(1,M+1):
   ang=2*np.pi*m*idx/n
   c=np.cos(ang); s=np.sin(ang)
   V[:,2*m-2]=c
   V[:,2*m-1]=s
return V
```
Potential n large M=24 -> 8192*48=393k floats ~3MB okay.

`def softmax_h(h):` as above.

`def fourier_eval(theta, V, tau_rel, n):`
- `h=V.dot(theta)`
- `a=softmax_h(h)`
- `c=conv_linear(a)`
- `cmax=float(c.max())`
- if cmax <=0 return inf
- tau_abs=cmax*tau_rel
- z=(c-cmax)/tau_abs; shift = z.max() (0)
- sumexp=np.exp(z - z.max()).sum()
- L=cmax + tau_rel*cmax*(np.log(sumexp) - z.max()) but z.max=0. Use `logsum = np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z)))` safe because z<=0, no overflow; underflow okay. If tau_rel>0.
- score=2*n*L
- q: if tau_rel <=1e-8: q top1; else q=np.exp(z); q/=q.sum()
- D=grad_D(a,q,n)
- weighted=float(np.dot(a,D))
- grad_h=a*D - a*weighted
- grad_theta=V.T.dot(grad_h)
- return score, grad_theta, a

Need `grad_D` uses q length 2n-1 and a length n. For q from weights, if c has small negative due FFT, max maybe okay. q sum maybe if all underflow? z<=0, max z=0 at peaks, at least one exp=1, sum>=1.

`fourier_grad_theta` same.

`def fourier_adam(n, M, time_budget, rng, deadline, tau_list, steps, start_scale, max_theta, lr):`
Implementation:
```
V=build_fourier_matrix(n,M)
best_score=np.inf; best_a=None
local_deadline=time.monotonic()+time_budget
start_scale_start=start_scale
start_count=0
while time.monotonic() < local_deadline - 5 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 5:
    theta=rng.normal(0, start_scale, size=2*M)
    # Adam state per tau
    for tau in tau_list:
       m=np.zeros_like(theta); v=np.zeros_like(theta); t=0
       b1,b2=0.9,0.999; eps=1e-8
       # reduce lr as tau decreases? use lr
       for step in range(steps):
          if time.monotonic()>local_deadline-2 or time.monotonic()>deadline-5: break
          sc,gr,a=fourier_eval(theta,V,tau,n)
          if sc<best_score - 1e-12: best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy()
          m=b1*m+(1-b1)*gr
          v=b2*v+(1-b2)*gr*gr
          t+=1
          mhat=m/(1-b1**t); vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
          # adaptive lr: base_lr / (1+0.01*t)
          theta = theta - lr * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
          theta = np.clip(theta, -max_theta, max_theta)
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)): theta=0
       # maybe before next tau, keep theta? continue; may help.
    start_count+=1
    # if start_count % 5 ==0 and no improvement for 3 starts? track.
    if start_count % 10 ==0 and best_score < 1.6: maybe stop? no.
    if start_count >= max_starts or no improvement many: break
```
Need pass max_starts. We can decide based on time. Simpler: while time. The start_scale can grow with start_count for diversity: `scale = 0.3 + 0.05*min(start_count,10) + 0.1*rng.random()` maybe. For very large scale, a peaked; okay. Use up to 2.0.

But LSE objective with high scale can be chaotic; Adam lr maybe. We'll use max_theta=3.0, start_scale=0.5 + min(0.15*start_count,1.0).

`fourier_adam` return a list of top? It can return best_a. But we might want multiple candidates from different starts, not just best. Could internally maintain `results` list of (score,a) when new best starts? Simpler: return best. Main will use.

But if initial best from Fourier remains local, multiple starts may produce different basin. We can collect whenever start's final score < some threshold or lower than current. We can return top K by maintaining a small list and replacing worst. Top-level function `add_to_candidates(cands, a, score)` where cands list of tuples; keep sorted max 5.

Main can call and add candidates.

Candidates storage:
```
candidates=[]
def consider(candidates, a, label): # top-level no closure
   if a is None or a.size<2: return
   a=normalize_seq(a)
   sc=score_exact(a)  # normalizes

`def shape_direction(a, c, target_frac, eps_frac):`
```
n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=get_fft_len(L)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
T=float(c.max())*target_frac
excess=np.maximum(c-T,0.0)
if np.sum(excess)<=0: return np.zeros(n)
delta=excess.copy()
delta -= delta.sum()/L  # sum zero
# Lowpass delta in Fourier to reduce high freq
Dh=np.fft.rfft(delta,N)
freqs=np.arange(N//2+1)
f_cutoff = max(1.0, N/20.0)
mask=np.exp(-0.5*(freqs/f_cutoff)**2)
mask[0]=1.0
Dh=Dh*mask[:len(Dh)]
denom=np.abs(A)**2
eps = eps_frac*denom.max() if denom.max()>0 else 1e-30
H=np.conj(A)*Dh/(2.0*(denom+eps))
h=np.fft.irfft(H,N)
h=h[:n]
# Remove mean? h may have nonzero mean, changing sum; step can include but normalize. To not waste mass, subtract mean of h? If subtract mean, first-order q DC? h mean not directly. But to preserve sum first-order, set h mean 0: h = h - h.mean(). Good.
h = h - np.mean(h)
return h
```
Does H Hermitian? Dh Hermitian, conj(A)Dh / denom real denom. As shown, H Hermitian if Dh corresponds to real delta and A real. Lowpass mask real symmetric. h real. Good.

`def shape_step(a, c, target_frac, eps_frac, alphas):`
```
n=len(a); cur=2*n*c.max()
h=shape_direction(...)
hmax=float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
if hmax<=1e-300: return a,cur
base_scale=a.mean()
best=a; bestsc=cur
for alpha in alphas:
    scale=alpha*base_scale/hmax
    a_new=a+scale*h
    a_new=np.maximum(a_new, floor)
    s=a_new.sum(); if s<=... continue
    a_new/=s
    c_new=conv_linear(a_new); sc=2*n*c_new.max()
    if sc<bestsc:
       best=a_new; bestsc=sc
return best,bestsc
```

Now, `run_mirror` with time budgets. Need manage `rng` and ensure deterministic but diverse. We can pass `seed` and create rng. For multiple mirror calls, use same rng sequential.

```
def run_mirror(a0, time_budget, deadline, rng, eta0=0.8, eta1=0.03, tau0=1.0, tau1=2e-4, topK_end=4, mom=0.5, stall_iter=400, use_shape=True):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    t_end=min(t0+time_budget, deadline-5)
    a=normalize_seq(a0)
    if a is None: return a
    n=a.size
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_exact(a)
    state=None; steps=0; last_best=0; stale=0
    shape_every=1000
    sym_check=200
    cur_score=best_sc
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
       c=conv_linear(a)
       cmax=float(c.max())
       cur_score=2.0*n*cmax
       if cur_score < best_sc - 1e-12:
           best=a.copy(); best_sc=cur_score; last_best=steps
       # sym every sym_check
       if steps % sym_check ==0 and steps>0:
           b=0.5*(a+a[::-1]); b=normalize_seq(b); sb=score_exact(b)
           if sb < best_sc:
               best=b.copy(); best_sc=sb; last_best=steps
           if sb < cur_score*0.999:
               a=b
       f = (time.monotonic()-t0)/max(1e-12,(t_end-t0))
       tau = tau0*(tau1/tau0)**f
       topK = None if f < 0.7 else topK_end
       eta = eta0*(eta1/eta0)**f
       q=make_weights(c,cmax,tau,topK)
       D=grad_D(a,q,n)
       Dscale=max(float(np.max(D)), cmax, 1e-30)
       state_new = D/Dscale
       if state is None: state=state_new
       else: state = mom*state + (1-mom)*state_new
       # Clip state to avoid extreme
       state=np.clip(state,0.0,50.0)
       a = a * np.exp(-eta*state)
       a=np.maximum(a, 1e-15/max(1,n))
       s=a.sum()
       if not finite or s<=0: a=best.copy()
       else: a/=s
       steps +=1
       if use_shape and steps % shape_every ==0:
           a2,sc2=shape_step(a,c,target_frac=0.92, eps_frac=1e-3, alphas=(0.2,0.1,0.05))
           if sc2 < cur_score:
              a=a2
           b2,scb=shape_step(best,cbest? we don't have cbest) ...
       if steps - last_best > stall_iter:
           # Perturb from best
           sigma = 0.08 + 0.08*rng.random()
           a = random_perturbation(best, rng, sigma)
           state=None
           last_best=steps  # to avoid immediate
           if use_shape:
               c=conv_linear(a) ... maybe not
    return best_sc,best
```
Need not sym inside every iteration because score_exact(b) uses FFT extra. Every 200 okay.

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline=t0 + max(30,budget_s) - 20
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    cands=[]
    # parent
    p=None
    try: p=globals().get('height_sequence_1') ...
    if p is not None:
       p=normalize_seq(p)
       add_candidate(cands,p,'parent')
       add_candidate(cands,symmetrize(p),'parent_sym')
    # decide n lengths
    if p is not None:
       n_parent=p.size
    else: n_parent=8192
    # initial n_main
    if budget_s < 600: n_main=4096; n_fourier=n_main; n_big=n_main
    else:
       n_main=8192
       n_fourier=4096
       n_big=12288 if hard_deadline - t0 > 1200 else n_main
    # add inits n_main
    add uniform, random smooth 4
    # quick mirror for top candidates? Maybe after Fourier.

    # Phase Fourier if time >180
    time_fourier = min(0.25*(hard_deadline-t0), max(60, ...))
    if time_left > 200:
       for M in [12,20]: ... but time split.
       a_four = fourier_adam(n_fourier, M, time_fourier_M, rng, hard_deadline, ...)
       resample to n_main and add; also add fourier length if n_fourier maybe 4096?
    # Also if parent resample to n_fourier? not needed.

    # Mirror n_main
    best_cand = cands[0] if any else uniform_n_main
    top = cands[:3]
    mirror_time_each = (hard_deadline - t0 - remaining_reserved) / len(top)
    For each cand in top:
       a=normalize; if len != n_main: a=upsample(a,n_main)
       sc,b = run_mirror(a, time_each, hard_deadline, rng,...)
       add_candidate(cands,b)
       # after each, if remaining small break

    # Big stage if n_big>n_main and time>400

       b=run_mirror(a_b, time_big, hard_deadline, rng, use_shape=True, stall=..., eta... maybe lower)
       add.

          b=run_mirror(best, time_final, hard_deadline, rng, tau0=tau_current? use small tau start, eta small, topK=8, use_shape=True)
       # Try sym final for bests:

    # final verify and return

Let's define `random_smooth_candidates(n, rng, count)`:
```
def random_smooth_candidates(n, rng, count):
    res=[]
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    for i in range(count):
       # random Fourier low frequencies
       theta = rng.normal(0,0.5,size=min(8, max(1,n//64)))
       h=zeros
       for m in 1..len(theta): h += theta[m-1]*cos(2π m x/n) maybe no sin? 
       a=exp(h-h.max()); normalize
       res.append(a)
    return res
```

`def make_simple_inits(n, rng):`
```
inits=[]
inits.append(np.ones(n)/n)
x=np.arange(n)/n
# Gaussian centered
for sig in [0.2,0.1]:
   g=np.exp(-0.5*((x-0.5)/sig)**2); normalize

# edge bumps

return inits
```

Potential issue: `np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)` where A is rfft complex. A*A not power spectrum; for real convolution, `A*A` (same orientation) is correct for linear convolution? For real sequences, ifft(FFT(a)^2) gives circular convolution of zero-padded a with itself. Since a zero-padded length N, circular convolution equals linear for length <=N. `A = rfft(a,N)`, full FFT conjugate at negative freq. Since `A*A` at positive frequencies corresponds to product; irfft reconstructs. Yes.

In `grad_D`, `np.conj(A)?` For linear convolution of two real sequences via FFT: ifft(fft(x)*fft(y)). For rfft, use product. No conjugate. We used `A*Q`. Good.

For shape H: We are solving in Fourier of linear convolution zero-padded length N. A=FFT(a). H=ifft(...). Need use `np.conj(A)`? We used H=np.conj(A)*D/(2|A|^2), which is least-squares solution for A H = D/2. But actual first-order q change in time domain: If h is vector first n coefficients, H=FFT(h) length N. Then Q_new = (A+H)^2 => dQ=2 A H. We want dQ_hat ≈ delta_hat (length N). So A H = delta/2. Least squares H = conj(A) delta/(2(|A|^2+eps)). Yes. This H corresponds to h possibly with non-zero coefficients beyond n? We take first n; H has spectrum consistent with full length N sequence h_full. Since a has zeros beyond n, h_full might have components beyond n, but we truncate h to first n (ignore beyond). The first-order dQ due to truncated h is 2 FFT(h_trunc) A; FFT(h_trunc)=H only if h_full beyond n zero; but our H may not have zeros beyond n. Truncation changes H. So h=ifft(H)[:n] has FFT not exactly H due to truncation? Actually if we set h_full of length N with h_full[:n]=h, h_full[n:]=0, its FFT is not H (which was solution for length N full). We are solving for h_full but then discarding tail, losing effect. This is a flaw. Need constrain h to have support in first n. The least-squares with support constrained is not simply H. We can instead use gradient D, which is physically correct. Shape step may be less effective due truncation. Could choose H and then set h=ifft(H)[:n]; the omitted tail means Fourier of h is not H; first-order change is not delta. But maybe still a useful direction. - `a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)`
- `s = float(np.sum(a))`
- if s <= 1e-300: return `np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size`
- `a = a/s`
- `floor = floor_base / max(1, a.size)`
- `a = np.maximum(a, floor)`
- `s2 = float(np.sum(a))`; if finite: a/=s2 else uniform
- return a

`softmax_from_h(h):` similar floor? If h inf? replace.
```
h=np.asarray(h); h=np.where(finite,h,0)
h-=h.max(); a=np.exp(h); s=sum; if not finite or s<=0 uniform; a/=s; floor=...; a=max; a/=sum; return a
```

`conv_linear(a):`
```
n=a.size
L=2*n-1
N=next_pow2(L)
A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
# clean
c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
return c
```
If n=1? L=1, N=1? `next_pow2(1)=1`. rfft length 1 okay. But we use n large.

`score_exact(a):`
```
a=normalize_seq(a)
if a is None: return inf
c=conv_linear(a)
m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
return float(2.0*a.size*m)
```
Since normalized sum 1.

`make_weights(c,cmax,tau_rel,topK):`
```
L=c.size
q=np.zeros(L)
if cmax <= 1e-300: q.fill(1.0/L); return q
if topK is not None and topK >0:
   k=int(topK)
   if k >= L: q.fill(1.0/L)
   else:
      ind=np.argpartition(c, L-k)[-k:]
      q[ind]=1.0/k
   return q
if tau_rel <= 1e-8:
   maxv=c.max(); ind=np.where(np.abs(c-maxv) <= max(1e-12*maxv,1e-300))[0]
   if ind.size==0: ind=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
   q[ind]=1.0/ind.size
   return q
tau_abs=cmax*tau_rel
z=(c-cmax)/tau_abs  # <=0
# numerical: clip to [-745,0] to avoid underflow? underflow okay but avoid nan
z=np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
q=np.exp(z)
s=q.sum()
if not finite or s<=0: uniform
else q/=s
return q
```
If c has cmax but due negative small, cmax positive. Good.

`grad_D(a,weights,n):`
```
L=len(weights)
Mlen = n + L - 1
N=next_pow2(Mlen)
rev = a[::-1]
A=np.fft.rfft(rev,N)
W=np.fft.rfft(weights,N)
full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:Mlen]
D=2.0*full[n-1:2*n-1]
D = np.where(np.isfinite(D),D,0)
D = np.maximum(D,0)
# maybe small negative zero
return D
```

`smooth_noise(n,rng):`
```
z=rng.standard_normal(n)
N=next_pow2(n)
Z=np.fft.rfft(z,N)
freq=np.arange(N//2+1,dtype=float)
sigma=max(1.0, N/16.0)
mask=np.exp(-0.5*(freq/sigma)**2)
mask[0]=1.0
Z=Z*mask[:len(Z)]
z=np.fft.irfft(Z,N)[:n]
z=z-z.mean()
std=float(z.std())
if std > 1e-15: z=z/std
else z=zeros
return z
```
Scale 1.

`random_perturbation(a,rng,sigma=0.2):`
```
z=smooth_noise(a.size,rng)
loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
loga += sigma*z
return softmax_from_h(loga)
```
If sigma too high, still positive.

`symmetrize(a):`
```
b=0.5*(a+a[::-1]); return normalize_seq(b)
```

`upsample_log(a,new_n):`
```
a=normalize_seq(a)
n=a.size
if n==new_n: return a.copy()
if n==1: return full new_n 1/new_n
loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,new_n)
lognew=np.interp(x_new,x_old,loga)
return softmax_from_h(lognew)
```
If new_n smaller, interpolation okay. Not used maybe.

`shape_direction`: We need `np.fft.rfft` of delta length L; N same as conv. Use A from a. `eps_frac` maybe 1e-2. Lowpass cutoff maybe N/32. If n small, cutoff at least 1.
```
def shape_direction(a,c,target_frac=0.92,eps_frac=1e-2):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1; N=next_pow2(L)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   if cmax <=0: return zeros
   T=cmax*target_frac
   excess=np.maximum(c-T,0.0)
   if float(np.sum(excess)) <=1e-300: return zeros(n)
   delta=excess.copy()
   delta -= float(np.mean(delta))
   Dh=np.fft.rfft(delta,N)
   freqs=np.arange(N//2+1,dtype=float)
   cutoff=max(1.0, N/32.0)
   mask=np.exp(-0.5*(freqs/cutoff)**2)
   mask[0]=1.0
   Dh *= mask[:len(Dh)]
   denom=np.abs(A)**2
   dmax=float(denom.max()) if denom.size else 0.0
   eps=eps_frac*max(dmax,1e-300)
   H=np.conj(A)*Dh/(2.0*(denom+eps))
   h=np.fft.irfft(H,N)
   h=h[:n]
   h=np.where(np.isfinite(h),h,0.0)
   h -= float(np.mean(h))
   return h
```
But target_frac close to 1 gives small excess only top max. If target_frac=0.95, top 5%. Good.

`shape_step`:
```
def shape_step(a,c,target_frac=0.92,eps_frac=1e-2,alphas=(0.2,0.1,0.05)):
   n=len(a); cur=score_from_c(c,n)
   h=shape_direction(...)
   hmax=float(np.max(np.abs(h)))
   if hmax <=1e-300: return a,cur
   best=a.copy(); bestsc=cur
   mean_a=float(a.mean())
   for alpha in alphas:
      scale=alpha*mean_a/hmax
      a_new=a+scale*h
      a_new=np.maximum(a_new, 1e-15/max(1,n))
      s=a_new.sum()
      if not finite or s<=0: continue
      a_new/=s
      c_new=conv_linear(a_new)
      sc=score_from_c(c_new,n)
      if sc < bestsc - 1e-12:
          best=a_new; bestsc=sc
   return best,bestsc
```
score_from_c normalized sum=1; c from a normalized. Good.

`shape_step` may be costly. Use alphas small.

def fourier_adam(n, M, time_budget, rng, hard_deadline,
                 tau_list=(0.35,0.12,0.04,0.012),

       # scale grows but capped

def add_simple_candidates(cands,n,rng):
   add_candidate(cands,np.ones(n), 'uniform')
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   for sig in (0.18,0.10):
      g=np.exp(-0.5*((x-0.5)/sig)**2); add_candidate(cands,g,'gauss')
   for width in (0.10,0.06):
      e=np.exp(-(x/width)**2)+np.exp(-((1-x)/width)**2)+0.02
      add_candidate(cands,e,'edge')
   # random smooth via low-dim V
   M=8
   V=build_fourier_matrix(n,M)
   for _ in range(3):
      theta=rng.normal(0,0.8,M*2)
      a=softmax_from_h(V.dot(theta))
      add_candidate(cands,a,'rand4f')

Range 2 to0 nonneg. q(τ)=∫ p(t)p(t+τ) for τ∈0..1 maybe. p(t)=1+cosπt. Product =1+cosπt+cosπ(t+τ)+cosπt cosπ(t+τ). Integral 0 to 1-τ. For τ=1: upper 0? Actually t+τ in [τ,1], if τ=1 no overlap ->0. For τ=0: ∫p^2 = ∫(1+2cos+cos^2)=2 + ∫2cos? ∫cos=0, ∫cos^2=1/2 =>2.5. Normalize? p∫=1, q(0) for continuous is 0 (point), but small τ? q(0+) from integral over zero? Need no lambda; manual top list:
```
def insert_top(top, sc, a, size=3):
   if len(top)<size: top.append((sc,a.copy()))
   else:
      # find index of largest score
      max_i=0; max_v=top[0][0]
      for i in range(1,len(top)):
          if top[i][0] > max_v: max_v=top[i][0]; max_i=i
      if sc < max_v:
          top[max_i]=(sc,a.copy())
```
Top-level.

`run_mirror` code exact:
```
def run_mirror(...):
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    if a is None:
        return (np.inf, a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_exact(best)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = min(t0 + time_budget, hard_deadline - 3.0)
    if t_end <= t0:
        return best_sc, best
    L = 2*n-1
    if use_dual:
        dual = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)/float(L)
    else:
        dual = None
    state = None
    it=0; last_best=0; last_sym=0; last_shape=0
    floor = 1e-15/max(1,n)
    # if uniform perturb
    if float(a.max()-a.min()) < 1e-5:
        a = random_perturbation(a, rng, sigma=0.25)
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        c = conv_linear(a)
        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        cur = score_from_c(c,n)
        if cur < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = cur; best=a.copy(); last_best=it
        # sym
        if sym_check >0 and it >0 and it - last_sym >= sym_check:
            last_sym=it
            b=symmetrize(a)
            sb=score_exact(b)
            if sb < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc=sb; best=b.copy(); last_best=it
            if sb < cur*0.999:
                a=b
                # maybe recompute c? continue? no, can continue with old c? Better continue
                c=conv_linear(a); cmax=...; cur=...
                if cur < best_sc ...
        # shape
        if shape_every >0 and it>0 and it - last_shape >= shape_every:
            last_shape=it
            target = shape_targets[(it//shape_every) % len(shape_targets)]
            a2,sc2 = shape_step(a,c,target,1e-2,(0.25,0.12,0.06))
            if sc2 < cur - 1e-12:
                a=a2
                c=conv_linear(a); cmax=...; cur=...
                if cur<best_sc ...
                continue
            # if not improved, maybe continue? no, proceed
        # stall
        if it - last_best > stall_iter:
            sigma = 0.08 + 0.12*rng.random()
            a = random_perturbation(best, rng, sigma)
            state=None
            if use_dual:
                dual[:] = 1.0/float(L)
            last_best=it
            continue
        # blend if bad
        if best_sc > 0 and cur > best_sc*1.05 and it - last_best > max(10, stall_iter//3):
            a = 0.5*a + 0.5*best
            a = normalize_seq(a)
            state=None
            last_best=it
            continue
        f = (time.monotonic()-t0)/max(t_end-t0,1e-9)
        if f<0: f=0; if f>1:f=1
        tau = tau_start * (tau_end/tau_start) ** f
        topK = None
        if f >= 0.7:
            topK=int(topK_end)
        eta = eta_start * (eta_end/eta_start) ** f
        q=make_weights(c,cmax,tau,topK)
        if use_dual:
            rate = dual_rate_start + (dual_rate_end - dual_rate_start)*f
            dual = (1.0-rate)*dual + rate*q
            ds=float(dual.sum())
            if ds > 1e-300: dual=dual/ds
            w=dual
        else:
            w=q
        D=grad_D(a,w,n)
        Dmax=float(np.max(D)) if D.size else 0.0
        Dscale=max(Dmax,cmax,1e-30)
        r=D/Dscale
        if state is None:
            state=r
        else:
            state = momentum*state + (1.0-momentum)*r
        state=np.clip(state,0.0,100.0)
        a = a * np.exp(-eta*state)
        a = np.maximum(a, floor)
        s=float(a.sum())
        if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s <= 1e-300:
            a=best.copy()
        else:
            a=a/s
        it +=1
    # final
    c=conv_linear(a); cur=score_from_c(c,n); update
    b=symmetrize(a); sb=score_exact(b); update
    return best_sc,best
```

Implementation of `fourier_adam` robust:
```
def fourier_adam(n, modes, time_budget, rng, hard_deadline, tau_scales=(1.0,0.35,0.12,0.04), steps_per_tau=120, lr=0.35, start_scale=0.45, max_theta=2.0):
    t0=time.monotonic()
    local_deadline=min(t0+time_budget, hard_deadline-3.0)
    V=build_fourier_matrix(n,modes)
    best_score=np.inf; best_a=None
    no_improve_starts=0; start_count=0
    while time.monotonic() < local_deadline and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline-5:
       scale = start_scale + 0.12*min(start_count,8) + 0.2*rng.random()
       if scale > 1.8: scale=1.8
       theta = rng.normal(0.0, scale, size=2*modes)
       before=best_score
       for tau_scale in tau_scales:
           tau_abs=tau_scale/float(n)
           m=np.zeros_like(theta); v=np.zeros_like(theta); t=0
           b1,b2=0.9,0.999; eps=1e-8
           for step in range(steps_per_tau):
               if time.monotonic() >= local_deadline or time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline-5: break
               sc, gr, a = fourier_eval(theta,V,tau_abs,n)
               if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                   best_score=sc; best_a=a.copy()
               m = b1*m + (1-b1)*gr
               v = b2*v + (1-b2)*gr*gr
               t +=1
               mhat=m/(1-b1**t); vhat=v/(1-b2**t)
               lr_t=lr/(1.0+0.002*t)
               theta = theta - lr_t*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+eps)
               if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
                   theta = rng.normal(0.0,0.5,size=2*modes)
               theta = np.clip(theta,-max_theta,max_theta)
           # Optionally, if theta is bad, continue
       start_count +=1
       if best_score < before - 1e-9:
           no_improve_starts=0
       else:
           no_improve_starts +=1
       if no_improve_starts >= 8:
           break
       if start_count >= 60:
           break
    # exact final best?
    if best_a is None:
       # use uniform
       best_a=np.ones(n)/float(n)
       best_score=score_exact(best_a)
    else:
       # exact score of best_a
       exact=score_exact(best_a)
       if exact < best_score:
           best_score=exact
    return best_score, best_a
```

def insert_top(top, sc, a, size=4):
    if not np.isfinite(sc): return top
    if len(top) < size:
        top.append((sc,a.copy()))
    else:
        max_i=0; max_v=top[0][0]
        for i in range(1,len(top)):
            if top[i][0] > max_v:
                max_v=top[i][0]; max_i=i
        if sc < max_v:
            top[max_i]=(sc,a.copy())
    return top
```

def grid_lowdim(cands,n,modes,deadline,grid_steps=15,rng=None,max_random=200):
    V=build_fourier_matrix(n,modes)
    top=[]
    if modes ==1:
       grid=np.linspace(-1.8,1.8,grid_steps)
       for x in grid:
         for y in grid:
           if time.monotonic() > deadline-2: break
           theta=np.array([x,y])
           a=softmax_from_h(V.dot(theta))
           sc=score_exact(a)
           top=insert_top(top,sc,a,4)
    else:
       # random
       for k in range(max_random):
          if time.monotonic()>deadline-2: break
          theta=rng.normal(0,1.0,2*modes)
          theta = np.clip(theta,-2.0,2.0)
          a=softmax_from_h(V.dot(theta))
          sc=score_exact(a)
          top=insert_top(top,sc,a,4)
    for sc,a in top:
       add_candidate(cands,a,'grid')
```

```python
"""
Optimizer for minimizing 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a)**2) over nonnegative
sequences.

Strategy:
- The objective is scale invariant, so all internal sequences are normalized
  to unit sum.
- A large part of the search is multiplicative/log-space descent in the
  sequence space, using LSE-smoothed peak weights and a slowly evolving dual
  vector over convolution lags.
- Low-dimensional periodic Fourier parametrizations are optimized with Adam
  to find globally useful smooth basins.
- Candidate sequences are refined, upscaled, symmetrized, and finally polished
  with very narrow peak weights and occasional frequency-domain shaping steps.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def first_elem(item):
    return item[0]


def next_pow2(m):
    r = 1
    while r < m:
        r <<= 1
    return r


def normalize_seq(a, floor_base=1e-15):
    if a is None:
        return None
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    a = a / s
    floor = floor_base / float(max(1, a.size))
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    s2 = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s2


def softmax_from_h(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if h.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h = h - float(np.max(h))
    a = np.exp(h)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(h.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(h.size)
    a = a / s
    floor = 1e-15 / float(max(1, a.size))
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    a = a / a.sum()
    return a


def conv_linear(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(c, 0.0)


def score_from_c(c, n, s=1.0):
    if c is None or c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0:
        m = 0.0
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def score_exact(a):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.inf
    c = conv_linear(a)
    return score_from_c(c, a.size, 1.0)


def make_weights(c, cmax, tau_abs, topK=None):
    L = int(c.size)
    q = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if L == 0:
        return q
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
        q.fill(1.0 / L)
        return q

    if topK is not None and topK > 0:
        k = int(topK)
        if k >= L:
            q.fill(1.0 / L)
        else:
            ind = np.argpartition(c, L - k)[-k:]
            q[ind] = 1.0 / float(k)
        return q

    if tau_abs is None or tau_abs <= 1e-300:
        vals = c - cmax
        tol = max(1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(cmax)), 1e-300)
        ind = np.where(vals <= tol)[0]
        if ind.size == 0:
            ind = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
        q[ind] = 1.0 / float(ind.size)
        return q

    z = (c - cmax) / tau_abs
    z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
    q = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(q))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        q.fill(1.0 / L)
    else:
        q = q / s
    return q


def grad_D(a, weights, n):
    """Gradient of weighted sum of convolution coefficients w.r.t. a."""
    L = int(weights.size)
    Mlen = n + L - 1
    N = next_pow2(Mlen)
    rev = a[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(rev, N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(weights, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:Mlen]
    D = 2.0 * full[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    D = np.where(np.isfinite(D), D, 0.0)
    return np.maximum(D, 0.0)


def smooth_noise(n, rng):
    z = rng.standard_normal(n)
    N = next_pow2(n)
    Z = np.fft.rfft(z, N)
    freq = np.arange(N // 2 + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    sigma = max(1.0, N / 16.0)
    mask = np.exp(-0.5 * (freq / sigma) ** 2)
    mask[0] = 1.0
    Z = Z * mask[:len(Z)]
    z = np.fft.irfft(Z, N)[:n]
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    std = float(np.std(z))
    if std > 1e-15:
        z = z / std
    else:
        z = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return z


def random_perturbation(a, rng, sigma=0.2):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    z = smooth_noise(a.size, rng)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    loga = loga + sigma * z
    return softmax_from_h(loga)


def symmetrize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
    return normalize_seq(b)


def upsample_log(a, new_n):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.ones(int(new_n), dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    n = int(a.size)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if n == new_n:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(new_n, dtype=np.float64) / float(new_n)
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, new_n)
    lognew = np.interp(x_new, x_old, loga)
    return softmax_from_h(lognew)


def shape_direction(a, c, target_frac=0.92, eps_frac=1e-2):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_pow2(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    T = cmax * target_frac
    excess = np.maximum(c - T, 0.0)
    se = float(np.sum(excess))
    if se <= 1e-300:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    delta = excess.copy()
    delta -= float(np.mean(delta))
    Dh = np.fft.rfft(delta, N)

    freq = np.arange(N // 2 + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cutoff = max(1.0, N / 32.0)
    mask = np.exp(-0.5 * (freq / cutoff) ** 2)
    mask[0] = 1.0
    Dh = Dh * mask[:len(Dh)]

    denom = np.abs(A) ** 2
    dmax = float(np.max(denom)) if denom.size else 0.0
    eps = eps_frac * max(dmax, 1e-300)
    H = np.conj(A) * Dh / (2.0 * (denom + eps))
    h = np.fft.irfft(H, N)[:n]
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h -= float(np.mean(h))
    return h


def shape_step(a, c, target_frac=0.92, eps_frac=1e-2, alphas=(0.25, 0.12, 0.06)):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    cur = score_from_c(c, n, 1.0)
    h = shape_direction(a, c, target_frac, eps_frac)
    hmax = float(np.max(np.abs(h))) if h.size else 0.0
    if hmax <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(hmax):
        return a, cur

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = cur
    mean_a = float(np.mean(a))
    floor = 1e-15 / float(max(1, n))

    for alpha in alphas:
        scale = alpha * mean_a / hmax
        a_new = a + scale * h
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, floor)
        s = float(np.sum(a_new))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            continue
        a_new = a_new / s
        c_new = conv_linear(a_new)
        sc = score_from_c(c_new, n, 1.0)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best = a_new
            best_sc = sc
    return best, best_sc


def run_mirror(a0, time_budget, hard_deadline, rng,
               eta_start=0.70, eta_end=0.02,
               tau_start=1.20, tau_end=2.0e-4,
               topK_end=4,
               momentum=0.5,
               stall_iter=400,
               use_dual=True,
               dual_rate_start=0.35,
               dual_rate_end=0.05,
               shape_every=1500,
               shape_targets=(0.95, 0.90, 0.85),
               sym_check=160):
    a = normalize_seq(a0)
    if a is None:
        return np.inf, a0
    n = int(a.size)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_exact(best)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = min(t0 + float(time_budget), hard_deadline - 3.0)
    if t_end <= t0:
        return best_sc, best

    L = 2 * n - 1
    if use_dual:
        dual = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
    else:
        dual = None

    state = None
    it = 0
    last_best = 0
    last_sym = 0
    last_shape = 0
    floor = 1e-15 / float(max(1, n))

    if float(np.max(a) - np.min(a)) < 1e-5:
        a = random_perturbation(a, rng, sigma=0.25)

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        c = conv_linear(a)
        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        cur = score_from_c(c, n, 1.0)

        if cur < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = cur
            best = a.copy()
            last_best = it

        if sym_check > 0 and it > 0 and it - last_sym >= sym_check:
            last_sym = it
            b = symmetrize(a)
            sb = score_exact(b)
            if sb < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = sb
                best = b.copy()
                last_best = it
            if sb < cur * 0.999:
                a = b
                c = conv_linear(a)
                cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
                cur = score_from_c(c, n, 1.0)
                if cur < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = cur
                    best = a.copy()
                    last_best = it

        if shape_every > 0 and it > 0 and it - last_shape >= shape_every:
            last_shape = it
            target = shape_targets[(it // shape_every) % len(shape_targets)]
            a2, sc2 = shape_step(a, c, target, 1e-2, (0.25, 0.12, 0.06))
            if sc2 < cur - 1e-12:
                a = a2
                c = conv_linear(a)
                cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
                cur = score_from_c(c, n, 1.0)
                if cur < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best_sc = cur
                    best = a.copy()
                    last_best = it
                continue

        if it - last_best > stall_iter:
            sigma = 0.08 + 0.12 * rng.random()
            a = random_perturbation(best, rng, sigma)
            state = None
            if use_dual:
                dual[:] = 1.0 / float(L)
            last_best = it
            continue

        if best_sc > 0 and cur > best_sc * 1.05 and it - last_best > max(10, stall_iter // 3):
            a = 0.5 * a + 0.5 * best
            a = normalize_seq(a)
            state = None
            last_best = it
            continue

        f = (time.monotonic() - t0) / max(t_end - t0, 1e-9)
        f = float(np.clip(f, 0.0, 1.0))

        tau_scale = tau_start * (tau_end / tau_start) ** f
        tau_abs = tau_scale / float(n)

        topK = None
        if f >= 0.70:
            topK = int(topK_end)

        eta = eta_start * (eta_end / eta_start) ** f
        q = make_weights(c, cmax, tau_abs, topK)

        if use_dual:
            rate = dual_rate_start + (dual_rate_end - dual_rate_start) * f
            dual = (1.0 - rate) * dual + rate * q
            ds = float(np.sum(dual))
            if ds > 1e-300:
                dual = dual / ds
            w = dual
        else:
            w = q

        D = grad_D(a, w, n)
        Dmax = float(np.max(D)) if D.size else 0.0
        Dscale = max(Dmax, cmax, 1e-30)
        r = D / Dscale
        if state is None:
            state = r
        else:
            state = momentum * state + (1.0 - momentum) * r
        state = np.clip(state, 0.0, 100.0)

        a = a * np.exp(-eta * state)
        a = np.maximum(a, floor)
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            a = best.copy()
        else:
            a = a / s
        it += 1

    c = conv_linear(a)
    cur = score_from_c(c, n, 1.0)
    if cur < best_sc - 1e-12:
        best_sc = cur
        best = a.copy()

    b = symmetrize(a)
    sb = score_exact(b)
    if sb < best_sc - 1e-12:
        best_sc = sb
        best = b.copy()

    return best_sc, best


def build_fourier_matrix(n, modes):
    n = int(n)
    modes = int(modes)
    V = np.zeros((n, 2 * modes), dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0 or modes == 0:
        return V
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    norm = np.sqrt(0.5 * n)
    for m in range(1, modes + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * m * idx / float(n)
        V[:, 2 * m - 2] = np.cos(ang) / norm
        V[:, 2 * m - 1] = np.sin(ang) / norm
    return V


def fourier_eval(theta, V, tau_abs, n):
    h = V.dot(theta)
    a = softmax_from_h(h)
    c = conv_linear(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(theta.shape, dtype=np.float64), a

    if tau_abs <= 1e-300:
        L = cmax
    else:
        z = (c - cmax) / tau_abs
        z = np.clip(z, -745.0, 0.0)
        e = np.exp(z)
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
            L = cmax
        else:
            L = cmax + tau_abs * (np.log(s) - float(np.max(z)))

    score = 2.0 * n * L

    q = make_weights(c, cmax, tau_abs, None)
    D = grad_D(a, q, n)
    weighted = float(np.dot(a, D))
    grad_h = a * D - a * weighted
    grad_theta = V.T.dot(grad_h)
    grad_theta = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_theta), grad_theta, 0.0)

    return score, grad_theta, a


def fourier_adam(n, modes, time_budget, rng, hard_deadline,
                 tau_scales=(1.0, 0.35, 0.12, 0.04),
                 steps_per_tau=120,
                 lr=0.35,
                 start_scale=0.45,
                 max_theta=2.0):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    local_deadline = min(t0 + float(time_budget), hard_deadline - 3.0)
    V = build_fourier_matrix(n, modes)

    best_score = np.inf
    best_a = None
    no_improve_starts = 0
    start_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < local_deadline and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 5:
        scale = start_scale + 0.12 * min(start_count, 8) + 0.2 * rng.random()
        if scale > 1.8:
            scale = 1.8
        theta = rng.normal(0.0, scale, size=2 * modes)
        before = best_score

        for tau_scale in tau_scales:
            tau_abs = float(tau_scale) / float(n)
            m = np.zeros_like(theta)
            v = np.zeros_like(theta)
            t = 0
            b1 = 0.9
            b2 = 0.999
            eps = 1e-8

            for _ in range(steps_per_tau):
                if time.monotonic() >= local_deadline or time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 5:
                    break
                sc, gr, a = fourier_eval(theta, V, tau_abs, n)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_a = a.copy()

                m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * gr
                v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * gr * gr
                t += 1
                mhat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
                vhat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
                lr_t = lr / (1.0 + 0.002 * t)
                theta = theta - lr_t * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)):
                    theta = rng.normal(0.0, 0.5, size=2 * modes)
                theta = np.clip(theta, -max_theta, max_theta)

        start_count += 1
        if best_score < before - 1e-9:
            no_improve_starts = 0
        else:
            no_improve_starts += 1

        if no_improve_starts >= 8:
            break
        if start_count >= 80:
            break

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_score = score_exact(best_a)
    else:
        exact = score_exact(best_a)
        if exact < best_score:
            best_score = exact

    return best_score, best_a


def insert_top(top, sc, a, size=4):
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return top
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if len(top) < size:
        top.append((sc, a))
    else:
        max_i = 0
        max_v = top[0][0]
        for i in range(1, len(top)):
            if top[i][0] > max_v:
                max_v = top[i][0]
                max_i = i
        if sc < max_v:
            top[max_i] = (sc, a)
    return top


def grid_lowdim(cands, n, modes, deadline, grid_steps=15, rng=None, max_random=160):
    V = build_fourier_matrix(n, modes)
    top = []

    if modes == 1:
        grid = np.linspace(-1.8, 1.8, int(grid_steps))
        for x in grid:
            for y in grid:
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2:
                    break
                theta = np.array([x, y], dtype=np.float64)
                a = softmax_from_h(V.dot(theta))
                sc = score_exact(a)
                top = insert_top(top, sc, a, 4)
    else:
        if rng is None:
            rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
        for _ in range(int(max_random)):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 2:
                break
            theta = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=2 * modes)
            theta = np.clip(theta, -2.0, 2.0)
            a = softmax_from_h(V.dot(theta))
            sc = score_exact(a)
            top = insert_top(top, sc, a, 4)

    for _, a in top:
        add_candidate(cands, a, "grid")


def add_simple_candidates(cands, n, rng):
    add_candidate(cands, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), "uniform")
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    for sig in (0.20, 0.12):
        g = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - 0.5) / sig) ** 2)
        add_candidate(cands, g, "gauss")

    for w in (0.12, 0.07):
        e = np.exp(-(x / w) ** 2) + np.exp(-((1.0 - x) / w) ** 2) + 0.02
        add_candidate(cands, e, "edge")

    modes = 4
    V = build_fourier_matrix(n, modes)
    for _ in range(3):
        theta = rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, size=2 * modes)
        a = softmax_from_h(V.dot(theta))
        add_candidate(cands, a, "rand4f")


def add_candidate(cands, a, label, max_keep=10):
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    if a is None:
        return
    sc = score_exact(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    cands.append((sc, a.copy()))
    cands.sort(key=first_elem)
    if len(cands) > max_keep:
        cands.pop()
    print(f"  candidate {label}: score={sc:.8f}, n={a.size}")


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(60.0, float(budget_s)) - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    print(f"Propose_candidate: budget={float(budget_s):.1f}s")

    cands = []

    p_parent = None
    try:
        obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if obj is not None:
            p = normalize_seq(obj)
            if p is not None and p.size >= 32:
                p_parent = p
    except Exception:
        p_parent = None

    if p_parent is not None:
        print(f"Found parent sequence length={p_parent.size}")
        add_candidate(cands, p_parent, "parent")
        add_candidate(cands, symmetrize(p_parent), "parent_sym")

    if float(budget_s) < 400:
        n_main = 2048
        n_fourier = 1024
        n_big = n_main
    elif float(budget_s) < 1000:
        n_main = 4096
        n_fourier = 2048
        n_big = n_main
    else:
        n_main = 8192
        n_fourier = 4096
        n_big = 10000 if float(budget_s) >= 1500 else n_main

    if p_parent is not None and p_parent.size != n_main:
        add_candidate(cands, upsample_log(p_parent, n_main), "parent_up_nmain")

    add_simple_candidates(cands, n_main, rng)

    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 250:
        grid_deadline = min(hard_deadline, time.monotonic() + min(45.0, 0.04 * remaining))
        if grid_deadline - time.monotonic() > 5:
            grid_lowdim(cands, n_fourier, 1, grid_deadline, grid_steps=15, rng=rng)
        if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 50:
            grid_deadline2 = min(hard_deadline, time.monotonic() + min(35.0, 0.03 * remaining))
            if grid_deadline2 - time.monotonic() > 4:
                grid_lowdim(cands, n_fourier, 2, grid_deadline2, rng=rng, max_random=120)

    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 350 and n_fourier > 0:
        fourier_budget = min(0.25 * remaining, 520.0)
        if fourier_budget > 60:
            modes = 16 if (n_fourier >= 2048 and remaining > 900) else 10
            print(f"\nFourier Adam: n={n_fourier}, modes={modes}, budget={fourier_budget:.0f}s")
            sc_f, a_f = fourier_adam(n_fourier, modes, fourier_budget, rng, hard_deadline,
                                     tau_scales=(1.0, 0.35, 0.12, 0.04),
                                     steps_per_tau=120, lr=0.35,
                                     start_scale=0.45, max_theta=2.0)
            print(f"  fourier adam best internal={sc_f:.8f}")
            add_candidate(cands, a_f, "fourier_n4")
            if n_fourier != n_main:
                add_candidate(cands, upsample_log(a_f, n_main), "fourier_up_nmain")

    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if p_parent is not None and remaining > 400:
        parent_budget = min(0.08 * remaining, 170.0)
        if parent_budget > 50:
            print(f"\nPolishing parent directly, budget={parent_budget:.0f}s")
            sc_p, a_p = run_mirror(p_parent, parent_budget, hard_deadline, rng,
                                   eta_start=0.65, eta_end=0.02,
                                   tau_start=1.1, tau_end=3e-4,
                                   topK_end=4, stall_iter=350, shape_every=1200)
            add_candidate(cands, a_p, "parent_polish")

    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 250 and len(cands) > 0:
        mirror_total = min(0.30 * remaining, 600.0)
        n_proc = min(3, len(cands))
        per = mirror_total / float(n_proc)
        print(f"\nMain mirror stage: n={n_main}, candidates={n_proc}, total={mirror_total:.0f}s")
        for idx in range(n_proc):
            if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 60:
                break
            a0 = cands[idx][1]
            if len(a0) != n_main:
                a0 = upsample_log(a0, n_main)
            sc_m, a_m = run_mirror(a0, per, hard_deadline, rng,
                                   eta_start=0.70, eta_end=0.02,
                                   tau_start=1.20, tau_end=2e-4,
                                   topK_end=4, stall_iter=400, shape_every=1500)
            add_candidate(cands, a_m, f"main_mirror_{idx}")

    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if n_big != n_main and remaining > 550 and len(cands) > 0:
        big_budget = min(0.30 * remaining, 700.0)
        if big_budget > 80:
            print(f"\nBig-length stage: n={n_big}, budget={big_budget:.0f}s")
            a_big0 = upsample_log(cands[0][1], n_big)
            sc_b, a_b = run_mirror(a_big0, big_budget, hard_deadline, rng,
                                   eta_start=0.55, eta_end=0.01,
                                   tau_start=1.0, tau_end=1.0e-4,
                                   topK_end=5, stall_iter=500, shape_every=1200)
            add_candidate(cands, a_b, "big_mirror")

    remaining = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 180 and len(cands) > 0:
        final_budget = min(0.25 * remaining, 320.0)
        if final_budget > 40:
            print(f"\nFinal polish, budget={final_budget:.0f}s")
            a_best0 = cands[0][1]
            sc_fin, a_fin = run_mirror(a_best0, final_budget, hard_deadline, rng,
                                       eta_start=0.32, eta_end=0.005,
                                       tau_start=0.55, tau_end=5e-5,
                                       topK_end=6, stall_iter=600,
                                       shape_every=900, sym_check=220)
            add_candidate(cands, a_fin, "final_polish")
            for idx in range(min(3, len(cands))):
                add_candidate(cands, symmetrize(cands[idx][1]), f"final_sym_{idx}")

    if len(cands) == 0:
        fallback = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        print("No candidates; fallback uniform.")
        return [float(x) for x in fallback]

    # Verify with the actual evaluator when possible.
    best_out = None
    best_verified = np.inf
    for idx, (sc_internal, a) in enumerate(cands[:2]):
        out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)]
        try:
            v = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            v = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(v):
            best_out = out
            best_verified = v
            break

    if best_out is None:
        best_out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in cands[0][1]]

    print("\nFinal length:", len(best_out))
    print("Final internal best:", cands[0][0])
    if np.isfinite(best_verified):
        print("Final verified:", best_verified)
    return best_out
```
